## Comments
### Comment: local/run#issuecomment-27 by @deepseek-3
Posted: 2026-09-29T06:00:27.26963994Z
Thread: 27 (open)
消费基线：origin/develop @ `698afd2`（应用代码 `0a08fdd`；脚手架、幂等种子、契约端点 1–6、ARIA 外壳已就绪）。
权威顺序：需求 = `input/requirements.yaml` 的 ATOMIC description（场景占位符损坏见 Issue #1 决定 D1）；契约 = Issue #1 comment #1 + v1.1（thread 1 comment #12）+ v1.2（comment #15）；视觉 = 仅决定布局与风格，不决定文案。
## 1. 判据（逐条，验收即按此驱动可访问名称）
- **REQ-1-2-1 新建空白工作簿**：首页按钮可访问名 `New blank workbook` → 创建页（提交按钮 `Create`）→ 成功后编辑器打开：**唯一**工作表 Sheet1、`aria-selected="true"`、A1 选中、6×26 全空；刷新与「回首页→重开」状态一致；失败时错误可见、停留可重试、首页不出现任何新记录。API：`POST /api/workbooks`。
- **REQ-1-2-2 重命名工作簿**：编辑器标题旁按钮 `Rename workbook` → label `Workbook name` 的文本框（预填当前名）+ `Save`；trim 后非空，空名精确文案 `Workbook name cannot be empty`；成功后编辑器标题与首页链接同步；失败错误可见且原名保留；重开显示最近保存名。
- **REQ-1-3-1 导入 CSV**：首页按钮 `Import CSV` → 对话框可访问名 `Import CSV`（文件控件 label `CSV file` + 按钮 `Confirm import`）；保持行列顺序、保留空字段、UTF-8 中英文数字、引号内逗号、成对转义引号、字段内换行；**字段以双引号开头且无闭合引号**为非法 → 精确文案 `Invalid CSV file format. Import failed.`；成功后工作簿名 = 文件名去掉末尾 `.csv`、Sheet1 打开完整行列与原文（首行是普通数据）；刷新/重开后不变；失败时首页无该名链接、不显示也不保留任何部分结果。
- **REQ-1-3-2 导出当前工作表**：编辑器工具栏按钮 `Export CSV` → 浏览器下载；建议文件名以 `.csv` 结尾、UTF-8 文本；按实际行列顺序保留**已用范围**内的空单元格；逗号/引号/换行正确转义；普通单元格导出显示值、公式单元格导出**当前计算结果**（C 依赖）；导出前后活动工作表、筛选视图、网格值、公式栏内容不变，刷新后仍一致。
## 2. 决定（D-A1…D-A10）
- **D-A1 创建页**：新增路由 `/new`。首页 `New blank workbook` 是 `<button>`（非链接），点击进入创建页；创建页含 label `Workbook name` 的文本框，**预填 `Untitled spreadsheet`**（逐字等于后端 `POST /api/workbooks` 省略 name 时的默认值，保证"直接点 Create"与"清空后重输"不产生名字分叉）+ 提交按钮 `Create`。空名（trim 后）**不发请求**，在页内显示 `Workbook name cannot be empty`，停留在创建页且输入保留；201 成功 → 跳转 `/workbook/:id`；失败（4xx/5xx/网络）→ 错误可见、保留输入、可重试，不发散任何记录。首页每次挂载重新拉列表（现有 `useEffect` 已满足），失败/成功后回首页不会看到半成品。
- **D-A2 重命名**：标题旁新增按钮 `Rename workbook`，打开 `role="dialog"`（可访问名 `Rename workbook`，`aria-labelledby` 指向可见标题），内含 label `Workbook name` 的文本框（预填当前名）+ `Save`。trim 后空 → 对话框内显示 `Workbook name cannot be empty`（前端与后端同文案，前端集中一处常量），对话框不关闭、标题保持原名；成功 → 标题更新、`updatedAt` 按 v1.1 语义更新；服务端失败 → 对话框内显示服务端 message，标题保持原名，可重试。首页链接在下次挂载时显示新名（不做客户端缓存）。
- **D-A3 导入端点（契约增量 A-1）**：新增 `POST /api/workbooks/import`，body `{fileName, csv}` → 201 + 与 `POST /api/workbooks` 相同形状的快照。服务端**单事务**内：`parseCsv`（`backend/src/csv.js` 纯函数）→ 派生名字（`fileName` 去掉末尾 `.csv`，大小写不敏感；trim 后为空回退 `Untitled spreadsheet`）→ 建 workbook + Sheet1（`last_active=1`、`last_selection=DEFAULT_SELECTION`）+ cells（`raw===''` 不落库，与既有惯例一致）。解析失败 → 400 `{error:{code:'INVALID_CSV',message:'Invalid CSV file format. Import failed.'}}`，事务不落任何行。允许同名工作簿（不发明唯一性约束）。
- **D-A4 导入结构尺寸（契约增量 A-2）**：`row_count = max(csvRows, 6)`、`col_count = max(csvCols, 26)`。理由：空 CSV 不得退化成 0×0（否则 A1 选区、网格渲染等不变量退化）；导入后的工作簿应保持"可继续编辑"——B 未合入前越界写整批 400，精确尺寸会让 2×2 导入无法在 D5 输入；下界取契约默认结构，不引入新维度语义。副作用：导入成为 `row_count/col_count` 的**第二个变更入口**（与 B 的结构端点并列），已作为契约增量报 #1。
- **D-A5 CSV 解析规则（`backend/src/csv.js`，纯函数 + 单测）**：分隔符 `,`；记录分隔 LF / CRLF / CR；末行换行与尾部空行不产生记录；空文件/纯 BOM → 0 记录；剥离开头 UTF-8 BOM；字段内容**原样保留**（引号内 `\r\n` 不规范化）；引号仅在字段第一个字符时进入引号模式，`""` → 一个字面 `"`，引号内 `,`/换行是字面量，闭合引号后的剩余字符按字面追加（`"ab"cd` → `abcd`，**不报错**）；未加引号字段中的 `"` 按字面字符处理；**唯一非法类 = 需求明示的"以 `"` 开头且到输入结束无闭合引号"** → 抛错。即"只拒绝需求明示的非法类，其余宽松接受"（见假设 A-5）。
- **D-A6 导入 UI**：首页 `Import CSV` 按钮打开 `role="dialog"`（可访问名 `Import CSV`，含可见标题）内 label `CSV file` 的文件控件 + `Confirm import`。文件用 `file.text()`（UTF-8）读取后以 JSON 提交（`express.json` 10mb 上限足够）。成功 → 跳转 `/workbook/:id`（与创建平行，满足"Sheet1 打开完整内容"）；失败 → 对话框保持打开、已选文件保留、对话框内 `role="alert"` 显示精确文案、首页列表不变；重选或再次 Confirm 即可重试。
- **D-A7 导出（客户端，无新端点）**：工具栏内按钮 `Export CSV` → `Blob([csv], {type:'text/csv;charset=utf-8'})` + `a[download]`；**无 BOM**、行尾 `\n`；建议文件名 `<工作簿名>.csv`（满足"以 .csv 结尾"）；导出不改变任何持久化状态（`updatedAt` 不变 —— 纯客户端导出天然免疫服务端 touch 副作用）。转义按 RFC4180：含 `,` `"` `\n` `\r` 的字段用双引号包裹、内部 `"` 双写。
  - **used range = 从 A1 到所有非空单元格的最大行/列包围盒（裁掉尾部全空行列）**；范围内的空单元格导出为空字段；整表无内容 → 空文件。理由：需求原文是"已用范围内"的空单元格；导出整个 6×26 声明结构会让空白/小表导出充满无意义逗号，并破坏"导出前后状态一致/可比较"的断言。
  - 已知边界（记录，不实现）见假设 A-7。
- **D-A8 显示值接缝（契约增量 A-3）**：新建 `frontend/src/lib/cellDisplay.ts`，导出 `worksheetDisplayValues(worksheet): (row, col) => string`；A 实现为**恒等**（返回 raw）。**网格渲染与 CSV 导出都经同一接缝取值**（`WorkbookEditor` 以 memo 计算后传给 `Grid` 的新可选 prop 与导出函数）。C 合入时把该函数换成引擎实现 → 网格与导出同时得到计算结果，结构上排除"网格显示结果、导出输出表达式"的分叉（C 自身的 REQ-4-1-1 就要求网格显示计算结果，因此该接缝必被接上）。C 合入前的过渡行为：公式单元格导出原文；A 的 e2e 先写该断言并明确标注"**C 合入时必须翻转为计算结果**"，整合 PR 另跑公式导出用例作为最终门禁。
- **D-A9 失败语义统一**：四类路径一律"错误可见 + 输入/文件保留可重试 + 无部分记录"；服务端单事务是"无部分记录"的防线，前端不重复实现原子性。精确文案集中在前端 `frontend/src/lib/copy.ts` 常量（`Workbook name cannot be empty`、`Invalid CSV file format. Import failed.`），后端保持既有字面量。
- **D-A10 组件与文件边界（降低与 B/C/D/E 并行冲突）**：A 新建 `WorkbookToolbar.tsx` 承载 `Rename workbook`/`Export CSV`，在 `WorkbookEditor.tsx` 只做**一处挂载**（复用现有 `role="toolbar"` 占位 div）；后续子 Issue 在该组件内追加控件（B 的结构操作、E 的 `Data` 菜单、D 的 `Undo`/`Redo`）。A 不改 `Grid.tsx` 的选区/编辑语义，只加一个显示值 prop。其余新增文件：`NewWorkbookPage.tsx`、`ImportCsvDialog.tsx`、`RenameWorkbookDialog.tsx`、`lib/csvExport.ts`、`lib/cellDisplay.ts`、`lib/copy.ts`、`backend/src/csv.js`、`e2e/workbook-lifecycle.spec.ts`。
- **视觉**：文案与可访问名一律用需求英文；布局风格按参考图（浅灰题头/工具栏、单一蓝色强调、扁平小字号、无重阴影）。参考图为中文界面且 `create-workbook.png` 只显示「＋ 新建」入口（visual 观察见附录 A），**不作为文案或创建页控件依据**；首页把 `New blank workbook` + `Import CSV` 放在工作簿列表上方（对应参考图的"新建"入口位置）。
- **A-1** `POST /api/workbooks/import` `{fileName, csv}` → 201 快照；`INVALID_CSV` 400 精确文案；单事务。
- **A-2** 导入是 `row_count/col_count` 的第二变更入口，尺寸下界 = 契约默认结构（6×26）。
- **A-3** 显示值单一接缝 `cellDisplay.ts`：Grid 渲染与 CSV 导出共用；C 负责替换实现。
- **A-4** 导出范围 = 已用包围盒（裁尾）、**无视筛选始终覆盖完整 used range**（E 的 REQ-5-1-2 要求导出包含隐藏行，与此一致）。
**Vitest（后端 `backend/src/csv.test.js` / `app.test.js`）**
- `parseCsv`：基础行列；空字段保序（`a,,c`）；引号内逗号；成对转义引号；字段内换行（含 CRLF 原样保留）；UTF-8 中文/英文/数字；CRLF 与 LF 记录分隔；末行换行不产生额外行；BOM 剥离；未加引号字段中的裸 `"` 按字面；`"ab"cd` 宽松接受；**未闭合引号 → 抛错**。
- `POST /api/workbooks/import`：成功（名字去 `.csv`、大小写不敏感、Sheet1 active、A1 选中、cells/结构正确、`updatedAt` 更新、首页列表出现、同名允许）；结构下界（空 CSV → 6×26；2×2 → 6×26；10×30 → 10×30）；非法 CSV → 400 精确文案且**DB 记录数不变**（无部分结果）；`fileName` 非字符串/缺失 → 400 统一错误体。
**Vitest（前端 `lib/csvExport.test.ts` / `components/*.test.tsx`）**
- `escapeCsvField`：逗号/引号/换行/CR 触发引号包裹与 `""` 双写；普通值原样。
- `worksheetToCsv`：裁尾包围盒；范围内空单元格保留；行尾 `\n`；空表 → `''`；**注入的显示值查找决定公式单元格取值**（证明 D-A8 接缝；恒等实现下等于原文）。
- 组件：创建页空名不发请求且显示精确文案；导入对话框非法 CSV 显示精确文案且不跳转；重命名对话框空名显示精确文案且标题不变。
**Playwright `e2e/workbook-lifecycle.spec.ts`（可访问名称驱动，生产形态，临时 DB）**
- REQ-1-2-1：首页 → `New blank workbook` → `Create` → 编辑器唯一 Sheet1 激活 + A1 选中 + 6×26 空网格 → 刷新一致 → 回首页出现链接 → 重开一致；失败注入（清空名字提交 / `page.route` 500）→ 错误可见、停留可重试、首页无新记录。
- REQ-1-2-2：`Rename workbook` → 预填当前名 → `Save` → 标题与首页链接同步 → 刷新持久；空名 → 精确文案、标题不变、首页仍旧名；服务端 500 → 错误可见且原名保留。
- REQ-1-3-1：`Import CSV` → 对话框 `Import CSV` + `CSV file` + `Confirm import` → 上传含中文/引号/换行/空字段的 CSV → 编辑器逐格断言（`exact: true`）→ 刷新一致 → 首页链接名 = 去 `.csv`；非法 CSV → 精确文案、首页无该名链接（刷新后仍无）、不跳转。
- REQ-1-3-2：`Export CSV` → 捕获 download，文件名以 `.csv` 结尾，内容 = 种子 `A1:C4` 的 4 行 LF 文本（裁尾包围盒）；转义用例（含逗号/引号/换行的单元格）；导出前后活动 tab、网格值、公式栏内容不变且刷新后不变；公式单元格：C 合入前断言导出原文（标注必须翻转），C 合入后改为断言计算结果。
- 复用 `checks/run-e2e.sh`（生产形态 + 临时 DB）；交付前跑 `pnpm -r test` + `typecheck` + `checks/run-e2e.sh`，证据留档（日志 + 候选提交）。
## 5. 假设与未决（保持可见）
- **A-5**：`"ab"cd` 等"有闭合引号但格式不标准"的字段按宽松接受处理（只拒绝需求明示的非法类）。若外部 harness 期望严格拒绝，会误判——需求文本不支持该期望，故取宽松。
- **A-7**：纯粹位于末行/末列之外的空字段（如 `a,b\nc,d,` 的尾部逗号）不在"已用范围"内，导出不保留；若根负责人要求导入→导出字节级 round trip，代价是记录"导入原始尺寸下限"（导出范围取包围盒与下限较大者），可增量实现。
- **导入成功落点**：按 REQ-1-2-1 的平行结构取"成功即跳到编辑器"（否则"Sheet1 打开完整内容"无法在首页满足）。
- **导出文件名**：取 `<工作簿名>.csv`；需求只约束 `.csv` 结尾。
- **A-3/A-4** 是跨 Issue 契约增量（影响 C 与 E），已在 #1 提出；若根裁定不同，按裁定调整，A 的实现改动是局部的（接缝一个模块、导出范围一段函数）。
- 外部 harness 的种子重置机制未知（沿用 Issue #1 已记录风险：不依赖重置端点、种子幂等）。
PR（base `develop`）承载实现计划、预演、实现与验收，接续 packet `tasks/issue-3-a/packet.md`。交接评论将给出 PR 编号与负责人，以及"哪些是必须由 PR 负责人补齐的证据"。
## 附录 A：视觉证据（vision 子代理，来源路径见 `input/reference/`）
- `create-workbook.png`：实际是中文云盘文件列表页（左侧导航 + 「＋ 新建」按钮被红框标注 + 文件名 `new sheet`），**没有** Create 按钮、没有名称输入框、没有对话框 → 创建页控件形态只能以需求文本为准。

- `worksheet-overview.png`：Google Sheets 形态编辑器：标题栏文件名、菜单行（文件/编辑/查看/插入/格式/数据/工具/扩展程序/帮助）、图标工具栏、名称框 `A1` + `fx` 空公式栏、空网格 A1 选中、底部单页签「工作表1」；**无可见的重命名/导入/导出控件**（菜单均关闭）。配色要点：白底 + 极浅灰题头/工具栏、单一蓝色强调（≈#1a73e8）、浅灰网格线、小字号无衬线。

4. **REQ-1-3-2 导出当前工作表**：工具栏按钮 `Export CSV` → 浏览器下载，文件名以 `.csv` 结尾、UTF-8、无 BOM、`\n` 行尾；used range = 非空包围盒（裁尾），范围内空单元格保留；逗号/引号/换行按 RFC4180 转义；普通单元格导出显示值；导出前后活动工作表、筛选视图、网格值、公式栏内容不变且刷新后一致（导出不更新 `updatedAt`）。

## 必须补齐的证据（候选提交上执行，保留首轮结果）

- 阶段: design 完成 → handover（本 packet 由 Issue 负责人建立，PR 接续同一 packet）
## 权威入口（不要另建副本）
- 设计与决定 D-A1…D-A10：**Issue #3 comment #27**（`braid issue comment view 27 --thread` 等价物 = `braid comment view 27 --thread`）
- 跨 Issue 契约增量 A-1…A-4：**Issue #1 thread 1 comment #28**（导入端点、导入是行列数第二入口、显示值单一接缝、导出 used range/筛选）
- 共享契约权威：Issue #1 comment #1 + thread 1 comment #12（v1.1）+ comment #15（v1.2：默认结构 6×26、gridcell 定位 `exact: true`）
- 需求权威：`/workspace/template/.factory26/20260929-042409-811f18d4/input/requirements.yaml`（ATOMIC description）
- 参考图视觉观察（仅布局/风格）：Issue #3 comment #27 附录 A
## 决定摘要（详见 comment #27）
- D-A1 创建页 `/new`：label `Workbook name` 预填 `Untitled spreadsheet`（= 后端默认值逐字）+ 按钮 `Create`；空名不发请求、页内精确文案；成功跳 `/workbook/:id`；失败保留输入可重试、无记录。
- D-A2 重命名：按钮 `Rename workbook` → 对话框（可访问名 `Rename workbook`）+ label `Workbook name` 预填 + `Save`；空名对话框内文案 `Workbook name cannot be empty`；失败保留原名；成功标题与首页链接同步、`updatedAt` 更新。
- D-A3 导入端点 `POST /api/workbooks/import` `{fileName, csv}` → 201 快照；单事务；失败 400 `INVALID_CSV` + 精确文案，无部分记录；允许同名。
- D-A4 导入结构 `max(csv, 6) × max(csv, 26)`；成为 `row_count/col_count` 第二变更入口（契约增量 A-2）。
- D-A5 CSV 解析规则：宽松接受非明示非法类；**只拒绝"以 `"` 开头且无闭合引号"**；BOM 剥离；字段原文保留；记录分隔 LF/CRLF/CR；尾部空行不产生记录。
- D-A6 导入 UI：对话框 `Import CSV` + `CSV file` + `Confirm import`；成功跳编辑器；失败保持对话框/文件、框内 `role=alert` 精确文案。
- D-A7 导出：客户端 Blob、无 BOM、`\n` 行尾、文件名 `<工作簿名>.csv`、不更新 `updatedAt`；used range = 非空包围盒裁尾；RFC4180 转义。
- D-A8 显示值接缝 `frontend/src/lib/cellDisplay.ts`，Grid 与导出共用；A = 恒等（公式导出原文），**C 合入时必须替换实现**；A 的 e2e 先断言原文并标注翻转点。
- D-A9 失败语义统一 + 前端文案常量 `frontend/src/lib/copy.ts`。
- D-A10 组件边界：新建 `WorkbookToolbar.tsx`（后续子 Issue 在其中追加控件）；`WorkbookEditor.tsx` 只做一处挂载；`Grid.tsx` 只加显示值 prop。
## 实现文件计划（PR 负责人细化）
- backend：`src/csv.js`（新）+ `src/csv.test.js`（新）；`src/store.js` 加 `createWorkbookFromCsv`（事务）；`src/routes.js` 加 import 路由；`src/app.test.js` 补端点/原子性用例。

## A-1 新增端点 `POST /api/workbooks/import`（A 交付）
- 请求 `{fileName: string, csv: string}`；响应 201 + 与 `POST /api/workbooks` **相同形状**的工作簿快照（`{id,name,updatedAt,worksheets:[…]}`）。
- 服务端单事务：解析 CSV → 名字 = `fileName` 去掉末尾 `.csv`（大小写不敏感，trim 后为空回退 `Untitled spreadsheet`）→ 建 workbook + Sheet1（`last_active=1`、`last_selection=DEFAULT_SELECTION`）+ cells。
- 解析失败 → 400 `{error:{code:'INVALID_CSV',message:'Invalid CSV file format. Import failed.'}}`，事务不落任何行（首页无该名链接、无部分结果）。
- 允许同名工作簿；`POST /api/workbooks` 形状**不变**。

### Backend
**backend/src/csv.js** — `parseCsv(text)` returning `string[][]`.
Rules (D-A5):
- BOM strip at start.
- Delimiter `,`.
- Record separators LF, CRLF, CR.
- Field content preserved verbatim (quoted `\r\n` not normalized).
- Quote mode only when field's first char is `"`.
- `""` inside quoted → literal `"`.
- Closing quote followed by remaining chars → appended literally (`"ab"cd` → `abcd`), no error.
- Bare `"` in unquoted field is literal.
- Only invalid: field starts with `"` and no closing quote until end of input → throw.
- Trailing newline / trailing empty lines don't produce records.
- Empty input / pure BOM → 0 records.
Let me design parser carefully.
```js
export class CsvParseError extends Error { ... }
export function parseCsv(text) {
  if (typeof text !== 'string') throw new CsvParseError(...);
  let input = text;
  if (input.charCodeAt(0) === 0xfeff) input = input.slice(1);
  const rows = [];
  let row = [];
  let field = '';
  let i = 0;
  let inQuotes = false;
  let fieldStarted = false;  // whether current field has begun (for quote detection)
  ...
Hmm, need to handle: "引号仅在字段第一个字符时进入引号模式". So when at start of a field, if char is `"` → enter quoted mode. In quoted mode, `""` → literal `"`; `"` followed by something else → close quote, then subsequent chars appended literally until delimiter/newline (they may include quotes literally? `"ab"cd` → `abcd`. But what about `"ab"c"d`? Per "宽松接受" — after closing quote, remaining chars appended literally. So `"ab"c"d` → `abc"d`? Let's just append literally until delimiter/newline. But careful: if after closing quote comes another `"`, like `"ab""cd"` → that's inside quotes (not closed yet) → `ab"cd`. Right: `""` is handled within quotes before treating `"` as closing.
Simplest state machine:

Actually let's implement with explicit functions:
  if (typeof text !== 'string') throw new CsvParseError('CSV content must be a string');
  if (input.startsWith('\uFEFF')) input = input.slice(1);
  const records = [];
  let record = [];
  const n = input.length;
  let atFieldStart = true;
  let quoted = false;     // currently inside a quoted section
  let sawQuoteStart = false; // field began with a quote
  while (i < n) {
    const ch = input[i];
    if (quoted) {
      if (ch === '"') {
        if (i + 1 < n && input[i+1] === '"') { field += '"'; i += 2; continue; }
        quoted = false;
        i += 1;
        continue;
      field += ch; i += 1; continue;
    if (atFieldStart && ch === '"') {
      quoted = true; sawQuoteStart = true; atFieldStart = false; i += 1; continue;
    if (ch === ',') { record.push(field); field=''; atFieldStart = true; sawQuoteStart=false; i+=1; continue; }
    if (ch === '\r' || ch === '\n') {
      // end record
      if (ch === '\r' && input[i+1] === '\n') i += 1;
      i += 1;
      record.push(field);
      records.push(record);
      record = []; field=''; atFieldStart=true; sawQuoteStart=false;
      continue;
    field += ch; atFieldStart = false; i += 1;
  if (quoted) throw new CsvParseError('unterminated quoted field');
  // handle final field/record: if input ends with newline, we already pushed; if field or record non-empty push
  if (!atFieldStart || field !== '' || record.length > 0) {
    record.push(field);
    records.push(record);
Wait: after a record separator, we reset atFieldStart=true, field='', record=[]. So at end of input if input ended right after newline → atFieldStart true, field '', record [] → don't push. Good.
If input is empty → n=0 → loop doesn't run; atFieldStart true, field '', record [] → no push → 0 records. Good (empty file → 0 records).
If input is "a" → field='a', atFieldStart=false → push ['a']. Good.
If input is "a," → after comma: record=['a'], field='', atFieldStart=true. End: atFieldStart true && field '' && record.length>0 (1) → push ['a',''] → 2 fields. Good (preserves trailing empty field).
If input is "," → record=[''], atFieldStart true, field ''; record.length>0 → push ['',''] . Good.

That's a trailing empty line producing a record with one empty field. Requirement D-A5: "末行换行与尾部空行不产生记录". Hmm. So "a\n\n" should produce just [['a']]? "尾部空行不产生记录" — yes.

"a\n\n" — trailing blank line. Should we drop it? D-A5 says yes, trailing blank lines produce no records.
Implementation: when encountering a record separator while the current record is "empty in progress" AND record has length 0 and field === '' and atFieldStart → this is a blank line. If it's a trailing blank line it's dropped. But middle blank lines? A blank line in the middle — e.g. "a\n\nb" — arguably an empty record. Google Sheets import would create an empty row. Actually a genuinely empty final record can only come from a blank line. So trimming trailing blank lines is safe: any trailing record that is exactly `['']` came from a blank line (or from the whole input being empty → no records). Wait, what if input is "\n"? Per "空文件/纯 BOM → 0 记录" and "尾部空行不产生记录", "\n" is a blank line at end → 0 records. Let me implement as: skip blank-line records entirely (a record that consists of a single empty field created by a bare newline). i.e. when we hit a record separator and `record.length === 0 && field === '' && atFieldStart` → it's a blank line; skip it (don't push). That handles mid-file blank lines too (skips them). Is that right? For a CSV with a blank line in the middle, CSV spec (RFC4180) says a blank line is a record with one empty field. But Google Sheets import of "a\n\nb" gives rows a, (empty), b. Hmm.
Trade-off: requirements only demand that trailing blank lines / final newline don't create records, and that empty fields are preserved. Skipping blank lines entirely could lose a genuinely empty middle row from a third-party CSV. But with `max(csvRows,6)`, most small cases are fine.
Better: keep blank-line records in the middle, drop trailing ones. Implementation: push blank records too, then at the end strip trailing records that are exactly `['']` (blank line). Let's do that: compute records, then `while (records.length && isBlankLine(records[records.length-1])) records.pop()`. But careful: this would also strip a legitimately-authored trailing empty row encoded as `\n` at end — which per D-A5 is exactly "末行换行/尾部空行不产生记录". Fine.
Hmm, but what about "a,\n"? That's ['a',''] — not blank → kept. Good.

{"content": "/**\n * CSV parsing for the import path (REQ-1-3-1, Issue #3 decision D-A5).\n *\n * The requirement names exactly one invalid input class: a field that begins\n * with a double quote but never closes it. Everything else is accepted loosely:\n *\n * - delimiter `,`; record separators LF, CRLF and lone CR;\n * - field content is preserved verbatim (a CRLF inside a quoted field stays a\n *   CRLF, it is never normalized);\n * - only the first character of a field can open quoted mode; inside quotes\n *   `\"\"` is one literal `\"`, and `,`/newlines are literal content;\n * - text after the closing quote is appended literally (`\"ab\"cd` -> `abcd`);\n * - a bare `\"` inside an unquoted field is a literal character;\n * - a leading UTF-8 BOM is stripped; an empty file (or BOM only) is 0 records;\n * - a trailing newline and trailing blank lines do not create a record.\n *\n * The parser is a pure function so both the import endpoint and its unit tests\n * can use it without a database.\n */\n\n/** Invalid CSV input (the single class the requirement rejects). */\nexport class CsvParseError extends Error {\n  constructor(message = 'Invalid CSV file format. Import failed.') {\n    super(message);\n    this.name = 'CsvParseError';\n  }\n}\n\n/**\n * Parse CSV text into a dense array of records (`string[][]`).\n *\n * @param {string} text\n * @returns {string[][]}\n * @throws {CsvParseError} when a field opens a quote that never closes.\n */\nexport function parseCsv(text) {\n  if (typeof text !== 'string') {\n    throw new CsvParseError('CSV content must be a string');\n  }\n\n  let input = text;\n  if (input.charCodeAt(0) === 0xfeff) input = input.slice(1);\n\n  /** @type {string[][]} */\n  const records = [];\n  /** @type {string[]} */\n  let record = [];\n  let field = '';\n  let quoted = false;\n  /** True while no character of the current field has been consumed yet. */\n  let atFieldStart = true;\n  let i = 0;\n\n  while (i < input.length) {\n    const ch = input[i];\n\n    if (quoted) {\n      if (ch === '\"') {\n        if (input[i + 1] === '\"') {\n          field += '\"';\n          i += 2;\n          continue;\n        }\n        quoted = false;\n        i += 1;\n        continue;\n      }\n      field += ch;\n      i += 1;\n      continue;\n    }\n\n    if (atFieldStart && ch === '\"') {\n      quoted = true;\n      atFieldStart = false;\n      i += 1;\n      continue;\n    }\n\n    if (ch === ',') {\n      record.push(field);\n      field = '';\n      atFieldStart = true;\n      i += 1;\n      continue;\n    }\n\n    if (ch === '\\n' || ch === '\\r') {\n      if (ch === '\\r' && input[i + 1] === '\\n') i += 1;\n      i += 1;\n      record.push(field);\n      records.push(record);\n      record = [];\n      field = '';\n      atFieldStart = true;\n      continue;\n    }\n\n    field += ch;\n    atFieldStart = false;\n    i += 1;\n  }\n\n  if (quoted) {\n    throw new CsvParseError();\n  }\n\n  // A record is still buffered when the input did not end with a separator\n  // (`a,b`) or when the last character was a delimiter (`a,` -> trailing empty\n  // field). An input that ended exactly on a separator leaves `atFieldStart`\n  // true with nothing buffered, which produces no extra record.\n  if (!atFieldStart || field !== '' || record.length > 0) {\n    record.push(field);\n    records.push(record);\n  }\n\n  // Trailing blank lines (`a\\n\\n`, `\\n`) are line noise, not records; a blank\n  // line is a record holding exactly one empty field.\n  while (records.length > 0) {\n    const last = records[records.length - 1];\n    if (last.length === 1 && last[0] === '') records.pop();\n    else break;\n  }\n\n  return records;\n}\n\n/**\n * Column count of a parsed CSV: the widest record. Empty input is 0 columns.\n *\n * @param {string[][]} records\n * @returns {number}\n */\nexport function csvColumnCount(records) {\n  let cols = 0;\n  for (const record of records) {\n    if (record.length > cols) cols = record.length;\n  }\n  return cols;\n}\n\n/**\n * Derive the workbook name from the uploaded file name: drop a trailing\n * `.csv` (case-insensitive), then fall back to the backend default when\n * nothing usable remains.\n *\n * @param {string} fileName\n * @param {string} fallback\n * @returns {string}\n */\nexport function workbookNameFromFileName(fileName, fallback) {\n  const stripped = fileName.trim().replace(/\\.csv$/i, '');\n  const name = stripped.trim();\n  return name || fallback;\n}\n", "path": "/workspace/template/.factory26/20260929-042409-811f18d4/braid-state/worktrees/pr-8/pi-deepseek-fast-g1/backend/src/csv.js"}

cellDisplay.ts:
import { cellKey } from './coordinates';
export type CellDisplayValue = (row: number, col: number) => string;
export function worksheetDisplayValues(worksheet: WorksheetSnapshot): CellDisplayValue {
  const raw = new Map<string, string>();
  for (const [row, col, value] of worksheet.cells) raw.set(cellKey(row, col), value);
  return (row, col) => raw.get(cellKey(row, col)) ?? '';
csvExport.ts:
import type { CellDisplayValue } from './cellDisplay';
import { worksheetDisplayValues } from './cellDisplay';
export function escapeCsvField(value: string): string {
  if (/[",\n\r]/.test(value)) return `"${value.replace(/"/g, '""')}"`;
export function worksheetToCsv(worksheet, display = worksheetDisplayValues(worksheet)): string {
  let maxRow = -1, maxCol = -1;
  for (const [row, col, raw] of worksheet.cells) {
    if (raw === '') continue;
    if (row > maxRow) maxRow = row;
    if (col > maxCol) maxCol = col;
  if (maxRow < 0 || maxCol < 0) return '';
  const lines: string[] = [];
  for (let row=0; row<=maxRow; row++) {
    const fields: string[] = [];
    for (let col=0; col<=maxCol; col++) fields.push(escapeCsvField(display(row,col)));
    lines.push(fields.join(','));
  return lines.join('\n') + '\n';

export function downloadCsv(fileName: string, csv: string): void {
  const blob = new Blob([csv], { type: 'text/csv;charset=utf-8' });
  const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
  const anchor = document.createElement('a');
  anchor.href = url;
  anchor.download = fileName;
  document.body.appendChild(anchor);
  anchor.click();
  anchor.remove();
  URL.revokeObjectURL(url);

export function csvFileName(workbookName: string): string {
  const base = workbookName.trim().replace(/[/\\:*?"<>|]/g, '_') || 'workbook';
  return /\.csv$/i.test(base) ? base : `${base}.csv`;

{"content": "import type { WorksheetSnapshot } from '../../../shared/types';\nimport type { CellDisplayValue } from './cellDisplay';\nimport { worksheetDisplayValues } from './cellDisplay';\n\n/**\n * CSV serialization for REQ-1-3-2 (Issue #3 decision D-A7).\n *\n * - used range = A1 to the furthest non-empty cell (trailing all-empty rows and\n *   columns are trimmed); empty cells inside that box export as empty fields;\n *   a worksheet with no content exports an empty file;\n * - records end with `\\n`, the file carries no BOM;\n * - escaping follows RFC 4180: fields containing `,`, `\"`, `\\n` or `\\r` are\n *   wrapped in double quotes and inner quotes are doubled.\n */\n\nconst NEEDS_QUOTES = /[\",\\n\\r]/;\n\n/** RFC 4180 field escaping. */\nexport function escapeCsvField(value: string): string {\n  if (!NEEDS_QUOTES.test(value)) return value;\n  return `\"${value.replace(/\"/g, '\"\"')}\"`;\n}\n\n/**\n * Serialize one worksheet to CSV text.\n *\n * @param worksheet the active worksheet snapshot\n * @param display the shared display-value seam (D-A8); defaults to raw text\n */\nexport function worksheetToCsv(\n  worksheet: WorksheetSnapshot,\n  display: CellDisplayValue = worksheetDisplayValues(worksheet),\n): string {\n  let maxRow = -1;\n  let maxCol = -1;\n  for (const [row, col, raw] of worksheet.cells) {\n    if (raw === '') continue;\n    if (row > maxRow) maxRow = row;\n    if (col > maxCol) maxCol = col;\n  }\n  if (maxRow < 0 || maxCol < 0) return '';\n\n  const lines: string[] = [];\n  for (let row = 0; row <= maxRow; row += 1) {\n    const fields: string[] = [];\n    for (let col = 0; col <= maxCol; col += 1) {\n      fields.push(escapeCsvField(display(row, col)));\n    }\n    lines.push(fields.join(','));\n  }\n  return `${lines.join('\\n')}\\n`;\n}\n\n/** Suggested download name; always ends with `.csv` as the requirement asks. */\nexport function csvFileName(workbookName: string): string {\n  const base = workbookName.trim().replace(/[/\\\\:*?\"<>|]/g, '_') || 'workbook';\n  return /\\.csv$/i.test(base) ? base : `${base}.csv`;\n}\n\n/** Trigger a same-origin browser download of the CSV text. */\nexport function downloadCsv(fileName: string, csv: string): void {\n  const blob = new Blob([csv], { type: 'text/csv;charset=utf-8' });\n  const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);\n  const anchor = document.createElement('a');\n  anchor.href = url;\n  anchor.download = fileName;\n  document.body.appendChild(anchor);\n  anchor.click();\n  anchor.remove();\n  URL.revokeObjectURL(url);\n}\n", "path": "/workspace/template/.factory26/20260929-042409-811f18d4/braid-state/worktrees/pr-8/pi-deepseek-fast-g1/frontend/src/lib/csvExport.ts"}

An empty (trimmed) name never reaches\n * the network: the exact requirement message is shown in place and the input is\n * preserved. Reuse that name on every command, explicitly with `--session <name>` when the environment is not preserved between shell calls. Parallel browser tasks in the same worktree need distinct names or prefixes. Pass the name when handing off the same journey so the next agent can continue with the same page and login state. Close only the named session you own (`agent-browser --session <name> close`). In a shared run, do not use `close --all`, even if a general CLI example suggests it; setting a session name does not scope that global operation. Sessions belong to browser tasks, independently of agent identities; use native profile or CDP options when the task needs them.

The exported CSV preserves empty cells within the used range according to the grid's actual row and column order and correctly escapes text containing commas, quotes, or line breaks. Ordinary cells export their displayed values; formula cells export their current calculated results rather than formula expressions. Before and after export, the active worksheet, filter view, grid values, and formula bar content remain unchanged, and the same state remains after refresh.\"\n\n设计决定 D-A7：客户端 Blob 下载、无 BOM、**行尾 `\\n`**、建议文件名 `<工作簿名>.csv`、不更新 updatedAt；used range = 从 A1 到所有非空单元格的最大行/列包围盒（裁掉尾部全空行列）；范围内空单元格导出为空字段；整表无内容 → 空文件；转义按 RFC4180（含 `,` `\"` `\\n` `\\r` 的字段双引号包裹、内部 `\"` 双写）。已知边界 A-7：纯尾部空字段（如 `a,b\\nc,d,` 的尾逗号）不保留。\n\n## 我的实现选择（待独立审视）\n1. 序列化时**每条记录后都写 `\\n`**，包括最后一条（即导出文本以 `\\n` 结束）。单测与 e2e 按此逐字断言（e.g. 种子 A1:C4 → `Region,Sales,Status\\nEast,1200,Open\\nNorth,800,Closed\\nSouth,700,Open\\n`）。\n2. used range 的包围盒由**原始非空单元格**（raw !== ''）决定；公式单元格 raw 非空，因此计入包围盒；显示值（接缝）只决定字段文本，不决定范围。\n3. 空表导出空字符串 `''`（零字节）。\n4. 转义只对 `, \" \\n \\r` 触发双引号包裹；**不**因子段含前导/尾随空格而包裹；字段内容原样保留（`\" a \"` 导出为 `\" a \"`，导入后 raw 为 ` a `）。\n\n## 待决问题\n- Q1：导出文本是否应包含末条记录后的 `\\n`？哪一种是更可辩护的阅读（需求只写了\"downloaded UTF-8 text is the exported CSV\"与\"preserves empty cells within the used range according to the grid's actual row and column order\"）？外部 harness 若逐字比对，哪一种更可能被接受/拒绝？是否存在能同时满足两种读法的行为？\n- Q2：以\"原始非空单元格\"而非\"显示值非空\"决定 used range 是否会在子 Issue C（公式求值）落地后产生不一致？举例说明是否存在\"显示值非空但 raw 为空\"或反之的真实情形。\n- Q3：转义策略（不因前导/尾随空格包裹）与 RFC4180 / 常见实现（Google Sheets 导出）是否一致？如果 harness 用\"解析回二维数组再比较\"而不是逐字比较，这四种情况（含空格、含 `\\r`、含 `\\n`、末尾换行）里哪些会导致不一致？\n\n## 我要的返回\n1. 每个问题的直接结论与理由；\n2. 支持结论的最强反例；\n3. 能改变选择的缺失事实（如果存在）；\n4. 若建议修改，给出最小改动点（我可以说出改动落在 `frontend/src/lib/csvExport.ts` 的哪个函数）。\n不要客套，不要复述我的材料。"}
